# Reading of the generation 5 comparison (implementer B, 2026-10-18)

Ben, 2026-09-14: "look at all of the output yourself and analyze if you think it is right or wrong and if not, iterate to improve it". The script's report follows this reading; the reading is of the script's "without" set (every V1 valid fact with no V2 fact over its cited bytes) and of the partials whose V2 counterparts together cover less than half of the V1 fact's cited bytes, each read against the V2 facts of the same section and the held facts' issue messages.

## Counts

| Measure | Generation 4 (V8, 2026-09-12) | Generation 5 (V9, 2026-10-18) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| V2 valid facts | 850 | 1,015 |
| V2 held | 145 | 22 |
| V1 facts with a V2 counterpart | 649 | 689 |
| V1 facts partial | 231 | 281 |
| V1 facts without a V2 counterpart | 102 | 12 |
| ... overlapping a fact V2 held | 84 | 11 |
| V2 facts new to V2 | 32 | 90 |
| Sections routed to a family | 93 of 104 | 104 of 104 |

Generation 5 carries 970 of the 982 V1 facts over the same bytes. The 90 facts new to V2 are 49 KEY_DEFINED_TERMS entries (42 of them the definitions section 8.3, the OTHER_DEFINED_TERM fact type the schema gained after V1), 38 REPRESENTATIONS in sections V1 routed IMMATERIAL (3.1, 3.5, 3.10, 3.22, 3.25, 3.26, 3.28, 4.4: organisation, no-conflicts, litigation, related parties, brokers, the fairness opinion, top customers), two MISC_BOILERPLATE and one PROXY_MEETING. Nothing in the new set reads as an invention; the exception-limb facts of 3.5 and 4.4 are the one pattern questioned below (defect 7).

Eleven of the twelve are (i): V2 extracted the fact and validation held it; one is (iii). None is (ii) a miss and none is (iv). The three V1 facts of 3.4 that V1 itself held are not in the set.

## The partials, read

Of the 281 partials, 226 have V2 counterparts that together cover less than half of the V1 fact's cited bytes. 144 of those are REPRESENTATIONS: in every one sampled (3.2, 3.12, 3.14, 3.15, 3.17, 3.18, 3.23, 4.3, 4.5) the V1 fact cites the whole sub-section, the exception chapeau ("Except as set forth in the specified Company SEC Documents or the Company Disclosure Letter, the Company represents and warrants ...") included, and the V2 fact's own words are the one limb that states the representation, with the chapeau inherited as context rather than cited as the fact's words. That is one limb of a whole-sub-section representation, not a loss; said once here, those 144 are skipped.

The other 82 (CAPITALISATION 24, TAX_MATTERS 12, CLOSING_CONDITIONS 12, EMPLOYEE_MATTERS 8, MISC_BOILERPLATE 7, GENERAL_COVENANTS 6, MAE_DEFINITION 5, INTERIM_OPERATING 3, MERGER_STRUCTURE_CLOSING 3, MATERIAL_CONTRACTS 1, NO_SHOP 1) were read one by one:

- 78 carry the V1 fact's substance in a V2 fact whose own words are the operative limb (a closing condition without its "The obligation of each Party ... is subject to" chapeau; a forum waiver limb "(c) that (ii) the venue ... is improper"; an MAE exclusion "(11) seasonal fluctuations in revenue or earnings"; a capitalisation absence "(C) subscriptions, options, warrants ..."). Not a loss. Within them, 30 sit under a different family in V2, always a representation V1 had filed under a covenant family (3.13 labour under EMPLOYEE_MATTERS, 4.6 listing under GENERAL_COVENANTS, 3.9 absence of changes under INTERIM_OPERATING, 4.12 Merger Sub under MERGER_STRUCTURE_CLOSING, 3.27 and 4.13 tax reps under TAX_MATTERS): V2 routes every representation under its article heading and places it on the representations table's row (Labor Matters, Taxes, Absence of Certain Changes), which is (iv) and consistent, not a defect.
- 2 are held V2 facts whose valid neighbour the script matched instead: 3.2 "no shares of Company Preferred Stock were issued and outstanding" and 4.2 "no Parent Ordinary Shares were held in treasury" are each a V2 fact held on "AMOUNT needs a canonical value" (defect 3).
- 2 are two V1 facts fused into one V2 fact: 3.27 and 4.13 "has not taken any action ... [and] knows of no fact, agreement, plan or other circumstance that would reasonably be expected to prevent ... the Intended Tax Treatment" is one V2 fact with two verbs (defect 6).

Ben's V1 comments on these partials are answered by the V2 output as far as the comparison can see: 3.1 ("key point here is just 'seasonal fluctuation in revenue of earnings' ... be clear with a marker that you have created a fusion provision from the intro then [...]") is the V2 exclusion fact whose own words are the limb and whose chapeau is an inherited component the page marks; 3.16 ("You should also inherit the limitations on the company SEC documents etc") is the inherited CHAPEAU origin on every representation fact.

## V2 extraction defects, ranked by count, with the fix proposed

1. **A context quote with a mistyped span id holds every fact of a section (9 V2 facts held, 7 V1 facts without counterpart, 3.4).** The nine 3.4 facts quote the Article III chapeau ("the Company represents and warrants to Parent and Merger Sub as follows:", "Except as set forth in (a) any Company SEC Document ...") with source_span_id `3d0f0a06...`; the closure's span is `3d0f7a06...` (one character), the only CHAPEAU span of 3.4's closure containing the quote, so work package 13 (a)'s relocation (finding (80), 17:06 UTC) resolves it; 3.4 was extracted at 15:21 to 15:27 UTC, before that rule reached the worker. Fix for these nine: re-extract 3.4 on the current worker (the manager's call, not run here). Rule still missing: an inherited context component (INTRO or CHAPEAU origin) that fails to resolve should be dropped with a note (COMPONENT_DROPPED_UNRESOLVED_CONTEXT) rather than hold a fact whose own components resolved, because the fact's words are its own components and the context is a reading aid. One line in `compileProposalComponents` (lib/product/agreement-draft.js) after `resolveEvidenceQuote`.
2. **A cross-reference to an Article does not resolve (3 V2 facts held, 1 V1 fact without counterpart, 6.4).** "this Article VI" is a CROSS_REFERENCE component; the resolver knows sections, not articles, so "2.0: CROSS_REFERENCE must resolve" holds each of the three per-party frustration facts. Fix: resolve a CROSS_REFERENCE naming an Article (or "this Article") to the article node the structure already has; a reference the resolver still cannot place becomes a `CROSS_REFERENCE_UNRESOLVED` note on a valid fact. `lib/product/fact-components.js` (the CROSS_REFERENCE rule) and the structure's article nodes.
3. **An AMOUNT with no number in its words is held (4 V2 facts held, 2 V1 partials: 3.2, 4.2, 2.1, 5.11).** "no shares of Company Preferred Stock", "no Parent Ordinary Shares ... in treasury", "the Maximum Cash Election Amount", "the Base Amount ... set forth in Section 5.11(b) of the Company Disclosure Letter" each fail "AMOUNT needs a canonical value". Fix: extend work package 14's rules from COUNT to AMOUNT: words "no", "none" or "zero" of the thing parse to 0 (`noneCount`), a bare defined term is `form: 'FORMULA'` with canonical null and a `VALUE_IS_FORMULA` note (`isFormulaValue`), and a value "set forth in Section X of the Disclosure Letter" is `form: 'DISCLOSED'` with canonical null and a `VALUE_DISCLOSED_ELSEWHERE` note; none of the three holds the fact. `lib/product/fact-components.js` `parseComponentValue`.
4. **A LIST of section references without elements is held (2 V2 facts held, 2 V1 facts without counterpart, 6.2, Ben's two comments).** "Section 3.9(c), Section 3.20, Section 3.21" came back as one LIST component with no LIST_ELEMENT children and the bring-down fact is held on "LIST without LIST_ELEMENT children". Fix: in the validator, a LIST whose text is a comma-and list of cross-references gets its elements made in code (one CROSS_REFERENCE child per reference, bytes from the text) before the rule runs; and the extractor line in the layer rules, "a list of Section references is a LIST with one LIST_ELEMENT per reference". The readout Ben wants (the standard: "in all respects", "in all material respects", MAE) is already in the two held facts' summaries; the conditions table's bring-down rows should code it as the tier column.
5. **A quote that is not exact holds the fact (3 V2 facts held, 1 V1 fact without counterpart, 4.7).** The three Form S-4 facts of 4.7 are PROPOSAL_EVIDENCE_NOT_EXACT with "byte range missing" on every component; 4.7 was extracted at 17:14 UTC. The words are the Parent SEC-document representation that crosses an SEC page footer in the canonical text (5.4's valid facts show the pattern: "the Transactions, 49 the Company"). Fix: `resolveEvidenceQuote` matches a quote with the page-footer tokens that `lib/product/source-display.js` already recognises skipped on both sides, and records the span over the footer; the display hides it as now. If the mismatch is elsewhere (the issue message names only the proposal), the re-extraction of 4.7 shows which.
6. **Two independently operative statements fused into one fact (2 V2 facts, 4 V1 facts: 3.27, 4.13).** "has not taken any action ... and does not know of any fact ... that would reasonably be expected to prevent ... the Intended Tax Treatment" is one V2 fact with two OPERATION components; OPERATING-RULES: "One fact states one independently operative legal effect." Fix: the extractor rule for representations, "two verbs the party represents separately (has not taken ... / does not know ...) are two facts, each with its own headline", with the check in `compileProposalComponents`: a fact whose top-level OWN components carry two OPERATION kinds joined by "and" gets a `FACT_MAY_FUSE_TWO_EFFECTS` note for the reviewer rather than a hold.
7. **Each exception of a no-conflicts representation becomes its own fact (10 V2 facts, 3.5, 4.4, 5.1).** "Securities Act, Exchange Act and blue sky filings are excepted from the Company's no-consents representation", "Certificate of Merger filing ... is excepted", "Nasdaq Global Select filings ... are excepted", one fact each, beside the no-consents fact they qualify. For MAE exclusions this is the layer rule Ben asked for (every carve-out its own row); for a representation's filing exceptions it is noise on the representations table. Fix: the REPRESENTATIONS layer rule, "an exception to a representation is an EXCEPTION component of that representation's fact, not a fact, unless the family's table has a row per exception" (`contracts/product/legal-schema.v2.json` overlay, built by `scripts/product/build-legal-schema-v2.js`); the table-shapes decide which families list exceptions as rows (MAE yes, representations no).
8. **A conditions chapeau extracted as a fact in V1 and not in V2 (1 V1 fact, 6.1).** Not a V2 defect: the chapeau states no condition. Listed so the set is complete.

Everything else the comparison shows as different is the schema (V2's one-fact-per-effect over V1's one-fact-per-sentence), the routing (V2 routes every section; V1 left nine IMMATERIAL) and the definitions section the V2 schema reads in full.

# V1 to V2 generation comparison: https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1692427/000119312526252096/d23867dex21.htm

V1: run eaafcac8-790b-41bb-a5e1-b12187a55e7d (LEGAL_SCHEMA/V1). V2: run 51577c57-41b1-4b75-bcdf-c84ef2558589 (LEGAL_SCHEMA/V2). Both generations share one agreement structure, so sections correspond node for node.

## Summary

| Measure | V1 | V2 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Sections routed to a family | 95 of 104 | 104 of 104 |
| Proposed facts | 1039 | 1037 |
| Valid facts | 982 | 1015 |
| Held by validation | 57 | 22 |
| Facts with a component tree | 0 | 1015 |

| V1 valid facts | Count |
| --- | --- |
| With a V2 counterpart (half or more of the cited bytes) | 689 |
| Partial counterpart (some cited bytes) | 281 |
| No V2 counterpart | 12 |
| ... of which overlap a fact V2 held | 11 |

V2 valid facts with no V1 counterpart (new to V2): 90 of 1015. Sections whose routing is the same in both: 46; differs: 58. The V1 review carries 38 decided or commented items; each is shown beside its V1 fact below.

## Why the two results differ

- The schemas differ by design: a V1 fact is one sentence with roles; a V2 fact is a headline over a tree of verbatim components with byte spans, laid out as coded tables. Counterparts are found on the shared bytes, not on wording.
- A section routed to more families in V2 (the routing widening by code and the V2 schema's subtypes) yields facts V1 never asked for; a section routed to fewer yields facts V1 had and V2 does not.
- A V2 fact held by validation (an unresolved component span, an invented role, an invalid tree) is not shown; a V1 fact over the same bytes then has no visible counterpart. The held fact and its code are listed under the section.
- The V2 tables place only facts carrying a readout; a V2 fact without one is listed under its section as evidence without a readout, with its V1 counterparts.
